The weighting is deliberately the inverse of ADR-0074's. There, the heaviest row was the backend partition (TC-ADMIN-070) because a missing tab is silent. Here the heaviest is **TC-ADMIN-074, which tests a test** — ADR-0074's partition was green while two real relationships rendered nothing, because it enumerates the same registry the derivation walks, so anything absent from that registry satisfies it vacuously. TC-ADMIN-074 is therefore asserted against `Base.metadata` instead, and is the one row in this file **mutation-checked** (the guard was inverted and confirmed to fail) — a completeness test that has never been seen to fail is exactly the artifact this story proves you cannot trust. See Test Design §63.

The heaviest row here is deliberately **TC-ADMIN-070, a backend unit test rather than a UI one** — the failure mode this story most needs guarding against is a relationship that should produce a tab silently producing none (nothing errors, the strip is just one short), which no assertion about the tabs that *are* rendered can detect. See Test Design §62 for why the partition is asserted in both directions rather than as a one-way spot check.

**Bidirectional junction listing ([ADR-0075](../adr/0075-junction-table-registry-completeness.md) Amendment 1, 2026-09-15):** **two new rows, TC-ADMIN-078 (P1) and TC-ADMIN-079 (P1)**, both in Taxonomy & Generic Admin CRUD, plus **TC-ADMIN-076 amended in place, not deleted** — its reverse-direction clause ("the reverse direction is an enumerated exclusion, not a servable relation") and its `test-cases`-tab-set clause are now false and are struck with the correction inline, while its zero-arm and both-arms `422` claims are still true and still assert. An amendment, not a supersession: the row's surviving half is the same assertion it always made.

**The recompute disagrees with incremental math, and the cause is findable.** Per `docs/CLAUDE.md`, the total below is a **direct summation of every row's literal value**, not "old total + this pass's delta." Incremental math would have given **307/175** (305 + 2 rows, 172 + 2 P1). Direct summation gives **311/178** — a **+4 / +4** disagreement, and none of it is this pass's own doing.

The attributable cause is the note directly above this one. **ADR-0075's own recompute was written but never applied to the table**: that note states "That area 58/34 → **62/37**, grand total **309/175**," yet the table still read `| Taxonomy & Generic Admin CRUD | 58 | 34 |` and `| **Total** | **305** | **172** |` — the four rows TC-ADMIN-074..077 were added to the document and counted in prose, and the two cells were never edited. So the Taxonomy row was carrying a **4-row undercount** before this pass touched anything.

A **second, smaller drift** sits underneath it and predates ADR-0075. Counting the actual table rows: Taxonomy & Generic Admin CRUD held **62** rows / **38** P1 immediately before this pass, where ADR-0075's note predicted 62/**37**. Its row count was right; its P1 count was one short, because the P1 figure it added to (34) was already one low. ADR-0075's note did re-sum the whole column first, exactly as the rule prescribes, and **still missed both** — which is the rule's own point restated: summing the stated literals verifies the *total against the rows*, and can say nothing about a *row against its own table*. That check is a row count, and it is what surfaced this.

Corrected here for **this pass's own row only**: Taxonomy & Generic Admin CRUD `58/34` → **`64/40`**, which is the literal count of `TC-ADMIN-*` rows in the table (64) and of their `P1` cells (40), verified by direct count rather than derived from any prior figure. Grand total **311/178** by direct summation: 34+39+21+14+16+12+8+**64**+5+25+29+32+12 = **311**; P1 22+23+11+10+11+7+3+**40**+4+4+16+19+8 = **178**.

**Found, not fixed — four other areas carry the same class of drift**, and correcting another story's rows inside this pass would be exactly the drive-by mass-fix the root `CLAUDE.md` warns against. Counting actual rows per section against the stated figures: **Auth** 35 vs 34 (+1), **Project & Release** 27 vs 21 (+6), **Requirement & Test Case Authoring** 19 vs 14 (+5), **Layout & Navigation** 39 vs 29 (+10) — struck-ID rows (the 5 `TC-LANDING-*` and `TC-DASH-005`) already correctly excluded from these counts, and the remaining eight areas reconcile exactly. A fully-reconciled table would therefore read **333/…**, not 311. Each of those four is almost certainly the same unapplied-recompute pattern found above and belongs to whichever story last edited it; flagged here so the next pass that touches one of those rows corrects it deliberately rather than inheriting it silently again.

**Relationship-tab write actions ([ADR-0076](../adr/0076-relationship-tab-write-actions.md), 2026-09-15):** **sixteen new rows, TC-ADMIN-080..095**, all in Taxonomy & Generic Admin CRUD. Backend: six P1 (065 declaration-completeness + its in-suite mutation checks, 066 declaration consistency and serialization, 067 create-then-read-back round trip on all four routes, 068 cross-tenant `404` on all four, 070 the `403`-not-`404` permission gate, 071 the standalone-`TestCase`→`TestSuite` regression asserted against **REQ-4's own route**) and three P2 (069 cross-project `422` + duplicate `409`, 072 the live migrated RBAC state, 073 idempotency by direct double `upgrade()` invocation). Frontend + e2e: one P2 (074 the `pathTemplate` substitution helper) and six P1 (075 the one-to-many **New** action and its locked scope field, 076 the many-to-many **Link existing** action and its declared-not-derived permission, 077 the two gates' independence + the refetch + the picker's three scoping cases, and 078/079/080 the three end-to-end browser flows — a 1-n create, a link through a **pre-existing** membership route, and a link through a **brand-new** traceability route). No row is struck through and none is amended — ADR-0076 supersedes nothing; it extends ADR-0074/0072 and their Amendment 1, every one of whose claims stays true.

Per `docs/CLAUDE.md`, the figures below are a **direct summation of every row's literal value**, not "old total + this pass's delta" — and this pass's own row was additionally **re-derived by counting the table itself** (`grep -c "^| TC-ADMIN-"` → 80) rather than by adding 16 to the previous figure, which is the check the note directly above found to be the one that actually catches drift. Counting `TC-ADMIN-*` rows in the Taxonomy table and their `P1` cells: **80** rows / **52** P1, which agrees exactly with `64 + 16` and `40 + 12` — so unlike the previous two passes there is no discrepancy to attribute here, and incremental math would have produced the same answer. Stating it anyway, because "the two agreed this time" is only knowable by doing both.

Grand total **327/190** by direct summation across every non-struck feature-area row: 34+39+21+14+16+12+8+**80**+5+25+29+32+12 = **327**; P1 22+23+11+10+11+7+3+**52**+4+4+16+19+8 = **190** (the struck-through Landing Page row still excluded).

**Found, not fixed — the same four areas the note above flagged still carry their drift**, re-confirmed by a fresh row count during this pass and deliberately left alone, since correcting another story's rows here would be exactly the drive-by mass-fix root `CLAUDE.md` warns against: **Auth** 35 actual vs 34 stated, **Project & Release** 27 vs 21, **Requirement & Test Case Authoring** 19 vs 14, **Layout & Navigation** 39 vs 29. The other nine areas reconcile exactly against their stated values. A fully-reconciled table would read **349/…**, not 327.

**100% automated and executed, against the isolated stack `testnexa-deve77ffc-test` (frontend `:34085`, backend `:38217`, Postgres `:41873`), 2026-09-15.** Backend unit **693/693** (`tests/unit/test_adr76_link_create_actions.py` 37 + `tests/unit/test_adr76_seed_trace_link_create_permissions.py` 7, covering TC-ADMIN-080/081 and the unit half of 072/073). Backend integration **16/16** for this story (`test_adr76_link_create_routes.py` 9 → TC-ADMIN-082..086; `test_adr76_link_create_permissions.py` 7 → TC-ADMIN-087/088), run against a live server on a database migrated to `3e6b08c5da71`. Frontend `tsc --noEmit` clean + Vitest **614/614** (`entityCrud.linkCreate.test.ts` 6 → TC-ADMIN-089; `EntityDetailPage.relationActions.test.tsx` 14 → TC-ADMIN-090..092). E2E `admin8-relation-tab-write-actions.spec.ts` **3/3** → TC-ADMIN-093/094/095, run twice (the second time capturing an end-of-test screenshot per flow as the live click-through record).

**Amendment 1's own coverage, same stack, same day.** Frontend `tsc --noEmit` clean + Vitest **626/626** whole-suite (`EntityDetailPage.relationActions.test.tsx` 14 → 26, the 12 new cases covering TC-ADMIN-096/097/098). E2E `admin8-relation-tab-write-actions.spec.ts` **4/4** → TC-ADMIN-093/094/095 plus the new TC-ADMIN-099, and the whole ADMIN-6/7/8 detail-page family re-run together as a regression sweep, **16/16**. No backend test changes: the amendment calls no new route and adds no permission code, so every backend row above still covers exactly what it covered. One non-test change rides along and is asserted by TC-ADMIN-096 — `components/atoms/card` now forwards `data-testid`, which it had been silently discarding (a hyphenated JSX attribute is not excess-property-checked, so the documented `entity-relation-actions` hook had never rendered).

**Full-suite integration failures classified, not waved through** (6 of 380, all reproduced identically on a solo rerun or proven pre-existing): four are the already-documented taxonomy-catalog state of this cloned database — `test_admin_testtype_seed.py::test_seed_presence_exactly_five_rows` (the missing `Confirmation Testing` row ADR-0075 already recorded as "found, not fixed"), its two siblings failing on `FileNotFoundError: 'alembic'` from a host-side `subprocess` call, and `test_admin5_seed_test_level.py::test_seed_produces_exactly_five_istqb_test_levels`, which sees **8 leaked fixture `TestLevel` rows** whose names identify their owners as ADMIN-2, ADMIN-7, PLAN-3 and REQ-5 — **none from this story**; the three rows this story's own first (cleanup-failing) run leaked were identified by tag and removed. The other two (`test_agents.py`, `test_planning_test_plans.py`) passed on an immediate solo rerun, the documented host-contention class. ADR-0076 touches no taxonomy code at all (`git diff --stat` carries no taxonomy file), so none of the four is attributable to it.
